1327	Degeneracy increases monotonically (6.4\% $\to$ 17.8\% $\to$ 47.8\%), and within-instance LP-quality correlation degrades in tandem (median $\rho$: 0.317 $\to$ 0.282 $\to$ 0.120), confirming that overtraining erodes both necessary conditions simultaneously.

Training duration modulates both instance degeneracy and within-instance LP-quality alignment. ``Degen.'': constant-F1 instance degeneracy (gold-filtered). ``LP $\Delta$'': LP selection gain over greedy (gold-filtered; none reaches the ${\approx}$1.5~pp MDE). ``$\rho$ med'': median within-instance LP-quality correlation.}

1349	\paragraph{Limitations.}
1350	The Few-NERD epoch analysis covers 2 points (3 vs.\ 5 epochs); the SciERC curve provides a 3-point gradient showing monotonic degeneracy increase with simultaneous within-instance alignment degradation.
1351	CoNLL epoch variation remains unverified; high-degeneracy datasets (CoNLL at 59--86\%) may exhibit a ceiling effect where additional training has limited marginal impact.
1352	All Few-NERD results in the main text use the 3-epoch model (standard early stopping); the 5-epoch comparison is a controlled ablation.
1353	All SciERC results use the 5-epoch model; the 3- and 10-epoch variants serve as controlled ablations.

{
  "recorded_role": "user",

      "content": "Entity Type & Degen.\\% & LP $\\Delta$ (pp) \\\\\n\\midrule\nTask & 10.1 & $+$2.58 \\\\\nGeneric & 10.0 & $+$2.07 \\\\\nMaterial & 20.0 & $+$0.91 \\\\\nMetric & 12.5 & $-$0.06 \\\\\nMethod & 16.3 & $-$0.03 \\\\\nOtherScientificTerm & 6.2 & $-$0.57 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\nThe cross-dataset degeneracy gradient (59.1\\% $\\to$ 17.8\\% $\\to$ 13.8\\%) is inversely associated with LP selection effectiveness at the macro level ($n{=}3$ datasets; exploratory).\\footnote{Partial Spearman correlation controlling for entity-type frequency yields $\\rho{=}{-}0.47$ (vs.\\ raw $\\rho{=}{-}0.38$); type frequency does not confound the degeneracy--LP relationship.}\nAt finer per-type granularity, selection depends on the interaction of degeneracy, oracle headroom, and type-specific LP-quality alignment.\nLow degeneracy is necessary but not sufficient: CoNLL MISC (23\\%, headroom 12.6~pp) and SciERC OtherScientificTerm (6.2\\%, headroom 12.5~pp) both show negative LP selection despite available headroom; LP correlation strength with the type-specific quality function is an additional prerequisite.\n\n\\section{Calibration Baseline}\\label{app:calibration_baseline}\n\nStandard calibration methods are monotonic transforms of log-probability and therefore preserve within-instance sample rankings: Platt scaling \\cite{platt1999probabilistic} fits a logistic regression $\\sigma(a \\cdot \\text{LP} + b)$, and temperature scaling \\cite{guo2017calibration} divides logits by a scalar $\\tau$.\nSince both preserve the argmax, selection F1 is identical to raw LP on all three datasets (Table~\\ref{tab:calibration}).\n\nTo break monotonicity, we fit a multi-feature Platt model (MF-Platt) using LP, token count, and entity count as inputs, trained on a held-out calibration split (20\\% of the test set, 5-fold cross-validation on the remainder).\nMF-Platt can reorder candidates within an instance, yet yields no significant selection improvement: $\\Delta{=}{-}1.1$~pp on SciERC ($p{=}0.72$), $+0.3$~pp on CoNLL ($p{=}0.31$), and $-0.6$~pp on Few-NERD ($p{=}0.83$).\nPlatt scaling substantially improves probability calibration (ECE drops from 0.26--0.39 to 0.01--0.08), but better-calibrated probabilities do not translate to better selection.\nThis parallels the supervised verifier result (\\S\\ref{sec:robustness_main}): neither stronger aggregation nor additional features can compensate for weak per-sample discriminative signal.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Calibration baseline results ($N{=}8$, $T{=}1.0$). Platt and TempScale are monotonic transforms of LP that preserve the within-instance argmax (selection F1 $\\equiv$ Raw LP, omitted). MF-Platt uses LP $+$ token count $+$ entity count; $p$: bootstrap test vs.\\ Raw LP.}\n\\label{tab:calibration}\n\\begin{tabular}{l cccc cc}\n\\toprule\n& & \\multicolumn{2}{c}{Selection F1} & & \\multicolumn{2}{c}{ECE} \\\\\n\\cmidrule(lr){3-4} \\cmidrule(lr){6-7}\nDataset & Greedy & Raw LP & MF-Platt ($p$) & & Raw & Platt \\\\\n\\midrule\nSciERC & .644 & .650 & .639 (.72) & & .260 & .048 \\\\\nCoNLL & .909 & .898 & .901 (.31) & & .392 & .012 \\\\\nFew-NERD & .771 & .785 & .778 (.83) & & .262 & .080 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\section{Epoch Confound Analysis}\\label{app:epoch_confound}\n\n\\paragraph{Training convergence modulates degeneracy.}\nTable~\\ref{tab:epoch_confound} compares the 3-epoch model (used throughout the main text) with a 5-epoch variant on Few-NERD (Qwen3-8B, $N{=}8$, $T{=}1.0$).\nExtended training increases greedy F1 by $+2.95$~pp (0.748 $\\to$ 0.777) but nearly triples instance degeneracy (11.1\\% $\\to$ 31.3\\%), reducing the fraction of instances with non-degenerate outputs from 85.6\\% to 68.7\\%.\nLP selection gains drop correspondingly ($+1.37$~pp $\\to$ $+0.62$~pp), consistent with the degeneracy-gating mechanism identified in the main text (\\S\\ref{sec:discussion}).\nWithin-instance LP-quality alignment is relatively stable under this convergence shift (mean $\\rho$: $0.524 \\to 0.407$; median $\\rho$: $0.667 \\to 0.615$), consistent with alignment being determined primarily by task structure rather than training duration.\n\nEvaluation loss increases after epoch 3 (0.032 $\\to$ 0.041 at epoch 5), and the best checkpoint by validation loss occurs at step 32{,}000 (approximately epoch 3.9).\nStandard early stopping practice thus naturally selects the low-degeneracy regime where LP selection is most effective.\nThis finding refines the narrative: degeneracy is not a fixed property of datasets but is modulated by the degree of training convergence. Over-trained models compress their output distributions, collapsing diversity even on datasets that support it under well-regularized training.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Training convergence confound on Few-NERD (Qwen3-8B, $N{=}8$, $T{=}1.0$). ``Degen.'': constant-F1 instance degeneracy (gold-filtered). ``Valid'': fraction of instances with non-degenerate outputs. ``$\\rho$ mean/med'': within-instance LP-quality correlation. The 3-epoch model corresponds to standard early stopping; the 5-epoch model is a controlled ablation.}\n\\label{tab:epoch_confound}\n\\begin{tabular}{lcc}\n\\toprule\nMetric & 3-epoch & 5-epoch \\\\\n\\midrule\nGreedy F1 & 0.748 & 0.777 \\\\\nInstance degen.\\ (\\%) & 11.1 & 31.3 \\\\\nValid instances & 3{,}723 / 4{,}351 (85.6\\%) & 2{,}988 / 4{,}351 (68.7\\%) \\\\\nLP $\\Delta$ (pp) & $+$1.37 & $+$0.62 \\\\\nWithin-inst.\\ $\\rho$ (mean) & 0.524 & 0.407 \\\\\nWithin-inst.\\ $\\rho$ (median) & 0.667 & 0.615 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\paragraph{SciERC epoch curve.}\nTable~\\ref{tab:epoch_scierc} extends the analysis to SciERC with a 3-point curve (3, 5, 10 epochs; Qwen3-8B, $N{=}8$, $T{=}1.0$).\nDegeneracy increases monotonically (6.4\\% $\\to$ 17.8\\% $\\to$ 47.8\\%), and within-instance LP-quality correlation degrades in tandem (median $\\rho$: 0.317 $\\to$ 0.282 $\\to$ 0.120), confirming that overtraining erodes both necessary conditions simultaneously.\nLP selection does not yield significant gains at any training duration ($-1.18$, $+0.62$, $+0.75$~pp; all below the ${\\approx}$1.5~pp MDE), despite oracle headroom ranging from 8.4 to 15.0~pp.\nAt 10 epochs, the model retains competitive greedy F1 (0.649 vs.\\ 0.650 at 3 epochs) yet both conditions are violated: 47.8\\% degenerate instances and $\\rho{=}0.120$.\nThe gradient confirms that degeneracy is convergence-driven rather than a fixed dataset property: extended training compresses output distributions, degrading both diversity and within-instance alignment. However, even at 3 epochs (lowest degeneracy, 6.4\\%), SciERC's within-instance alignment ($\\rho{=}0.317$) remains below the threshold observed on Few-NERD ($\\rho{\\sim}0.5$), reinforcing that controlling degeneracy alone is insufficient when the alignment condition is not met.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{SciERC NER epoch curve (Qwen3-8B, $N{=}8$, $T{=}1.0$). Training duration modulates both instance degeneracy and within-instance LP-quality alignment. ``Degen.'': constant-F1 instance degeneracy (gold-filtered). ``LP $\\Delta$'': LP selection gain over greedy (gold-filtered; none reaches the ${\\approx}$1.5~pp MDE). ``$\\rho$ med'': median within-instance LP-quality correlation.}\n\\label{tab:epoch_scierc}\n\\begin{tabular}{lccc}\n\\toprule\nMetric & 3-epoch & 5-epoch & 10-epoch \\\\\n\\midrule\nGreedy F1 & 0.650 & 0.644 & 0.649 \\\\\nInstance degen.\\ (\\%) & 6.4 & 17.8 & 47.8 \\\\\nLP $\\Delta$ (pp) & $-$1.18 & $+$0.62 & $+$0.75 \\\\\nWithin-inst.\\ $\\rho$ (median) & 0.317 & 0.282 & 0.120 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\paragraph{Limitations.}\nThe Few-NERD epoch analysis covers 2 points (3 vs.\\ 5 epochs); the SciERC curve provides a 3-point gradient showing monotonic degeneracy increase with simultaneous within-instance alignment degradation.\nCoNLL epoch variation remains unverified; high-degeneracy datasets (CoNLL at 59--86\\%) may exhibit a ceiling effect where additional training has limited marginal impact.\nAll Few-NERD results in the main text use the 3-epoch model (standard early stopping); the 5-epoch comparison is a controlled ablation.\nAll SciERC results use the 5-epoch model; the 3- and 10-epoch variants serve as controlled ablations.\n",

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have read the full paper including all sections, appendix, and references. Let me now compile my thorough review.

Let me organize my findings:

## Paper Summary
This paper evaluates five confidence signals (Soft Jaccard, Voting Confidence, Fleiss' κ, Exact Match, Log-Probability) for quality estimation and sample selection in LLM-based NER. The main finding is a "correlation-selection gap": signals can predict instance-level quality (QE) but fail to select the best output among N candidates. Two mechanisms are identified: instance degeneracy (identical F1 across samples) and weak LP-quality alignment. LP achieves +1.36 pp on Few-NERD when both conditions are met (low degeneracy <15%, alignment ρ>0.3).
 
## My Review Focus: Method rigor + Experiment design

Looking at the statistical methodology, the paper appropriately uses Spearman ρ for rank correlation and AUROC for binary discrimination. For the SciERC dataset with n=529 paired observations, the authors report a minimum detectable effect of approximately 1.5 pp at 80% power, which means effects smaller than 1 pp would lack sufficient statistical power to detect reliably.

On multiple comparisons, they apply Bonferroni correction to the 5 signal comparisons in the non-tied LP subset, and while the full analysis spans roughly 20 comparisons across different configurations, they justify this in the appendix by noting that since primary results are non-significant, more conservative corrections would only strengthen their null findings. The Few-NERD dataset with its much larger sample (n=32,565) provides a more definitive test with a smaller MDE of 0.22 pp.

Regarding the 2-seed design for primary results, the paper acknowledges this limitation by showing that three-seed experiments yield standard deviations of 0.026 or less, and they explicitly discuss how training seed variability affects their conclusions.

For causal inference, the cross-dataset gradient analysis with just three data points across different datasets, sizes, and training procedures is appropriately framed as exploratory rather than causal. The per-entity-type analysis shows a strong correlation (ρ=-0.881, p=0.004) but the paper correctly identifies that these entity types share the same model and tokenizer, reducing the effective degrees of freedom and limiting what can be concluded about independence.

The LoRA-rank ablation conflates capacity with diversity since lower ranks have fewer parameters, making temperature ablation the cleaner test. However, at T=1.2 the alignment weakens considerably—degeneracy sits at 12.5% (comparable to Few-NERD's 13.8%) but the correlation drops to ρ=0.225 versus Few-NERD's ~0.5, and the language modeling objective doesn't improve entity selection.

The claim that both conditions are jointly necessary rests on temperature ablation showing low degeneracy alone isn't sufficient, combined with LoRA-rank results and Few-NERD's success when both are present. The evidence is well-triangulated across datasets, but "jointly necessary" overstates what the data shows—it's more precise to say both conditions appear necessary within the tested regimes.

There's also a confound in the Few-NERD setup: it uses 3 epochs while other experiments use 5, which the paper does address with an ablation. epoch Few-NERD has higher degeneracy (31.3% vs 11.1%) and lower LP gains (+0.62 vs +1.37pp). But this means the primary Few-NERD result is at 3 epochs while the primary SciERC result is at 5 epochs, making the cross-dataset comparison confounded by training duration. The paper acknowledges this but doesn't fully resolve it.

**Thinking token confound**: The paper provides three arguments: LP shift is small (2.68% of median range), constant prefix cancellation preserves rankings, and CoNLL (without thinking tokens) shows higher tied fractions. This is a thorough analysis that adequately rules out this confound.

**Supervised verifier baseline**: The concern is that the verifier only combines the same 5 signals rather than learning richer representations like hidden states or entity embeddings. It's testing whether combining these unsupervised signals helps, not whether a truly learned verifier could close the gap—so calling it a "supervised verifier" slightly overstates what's being evaluated.

**Gold-empty filtering**: Appears consistent across both QE and selection, with the footnote showing that full-set Spearman ρ values are higher than gold-filtered ones, indicating the impact is documented.

**ε=0.05 threshold**: Clearly labeled as post-hoc exploratory with a full sensitivity sweep showing selection F1 is stable across the range tested, so this is adequately handled.

Now I'm looking at how degeneracy is actually defined—it's the fraction of instances where all N samples produce identical F1 scores, not identical outputs. The appendix shows these are different: 17.8% have constant F1 while only 11% have truly identical outputs, meaning there's a 6.8% gap where outputs differ but happen to get the same F1 score.

The paper does clarify this in the footnote to Table 4, though the term "degeneracy" could be clearer since it might suggest identical outputs to readers. The definition matters for the selection problem because what really matters is whether there's room to improve F1, not whether outputs are identical.

Let me verify the dataset numbers: SciERC has 529 gold-filtered instances (551 total minus 22 empty), and CoNLL has 2,756 (3,453 minus 697).

For Few-NERD, the paper reports 37,648 total instances with 32,565 after gold-filtering, removing about 13.5% of instances. The selection results check out too—SciERC's oracle-greedy headroom is 13.3 percentage points, and the slight difference between Table 3 and Table 4 for greedy performance is explained by the max-LP aggregation and single-seed evaluation approach.

The experimental design is thorough with extensive ablations and...

Now looking at the strengths and weaknesses: the paper excels at transparency about limitations and makes a solid methodological contribution with the QE versus selection distinction, backed by clear definitions and a detailed appendix. However, there are some real concerns—the Few-NERD versus SciERC comparison conflates different training epochs, the 2-seed design for primary results feels underpowered for generalization claims, and the per-entity-type analysis has non-independence issues that weaken the degeneracy-gating evidence. The "supervised verifier" framing is also somewhat misleading since it just combines the same five unsupervised signals, and the scope limited to 4B-8B models raises questions about practical value.

The causal claim about two jointly necessary conditions is overstated given the correlational evidence, and the findings may not generalize to larger models like 70B+.
```

{
  "text": "\n# Reviewer 1 — Round 1 审稿意见\n\n**论文**: Strong Predictors, Weak Selectors: Why Confidence Signals Fail to Scale Structured Information Extraction\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference (ACL Rolling Review)\n**审查侧重**: 方法严谨性 + 实验设计\n\n---\n\n## 1. Summary\n\n本文评估了五种置信度信号（四种 instance-level：SJ/VC/FK/EM，一种 per-sample：LP）用于 LLM-based NER 的质量估计（QE）和样本选择（selection）。核心发现是 \"correlation-selection gap\"：所有信号都能实现可靠的 QE（ρ up to 0.45, AUROC up to 0.82），但在 selection 上几乎无法超越 greedy decoding。LP 仅在两个条件同时满足时（低 degeneracy <15% + 足够的 LP-quality alignment ρ≳0.3）才能实现显著的 selection 增益（Few-NERD +1.36pp）。两个阻断机制被识别：instance degeneracy 和 weak LP-quality alignment。\n\n## 2. Strengths\n\n**S1. QE 与 selection 的系统性分离。** 这是本文最重要的方法论贡献。先前工作常将 \"信号与质量相关\" 等同于 \"信号可用于选择\"，本文用大量实验严格证明了这一等价关系不成立。这一洞察对实践者有直接价值。\n\n**S2. 极其透明的实验报告。** 几乎每一个有争议的设计选择都被主动标注并讨论：Few-NERD 3-epoch vs 5-epoch 差异（§4.1, Appendix N）、thinking token confound（Appendix F）、ε=0.05 的 post-hoc 性质（Appendix D）、per-entity-type 分析的 non-independence（§4.4, Contribution 3）、LoRA-rank ablation 的 capacity confound（§4.5）。这种透明度在同类实证论文中非常少见。\n\n**S3. 多层次证据三角验证。** \"两个必要条件\" 的论证通过多条独立证据线收敛：temperature ablation（改变 diversity 不改变 capacity）、LoRA-rank ablation（虽有 confound 但方向一致）、cross-dataset gradient（exploratory）、per-entity-type 分析（within-dataset）、epoch curve（SciERC 3-point）。单独每条证据都不足以支撑因果推断，但综合起来构成了较强的 converging evidence。\n\n**S4. Instance-level vs per-sample 信号的 definitional observation。** §3.1 指出 4/5 信号是 instance-level aggregates，by construction 无法区分同一 input 下的不同 candidates。这不是实验发现而是逻辑必然，为后续分析提供了清晰的结构性解释。\n\n**S5. 附录的深度和完整性。** 14 个 appendix sections 覆盖了几乎所有可预见的 reviewer 质疑：FK independence test、SJ normalizer robustness、ε sensitivity sweep、free-form decoding ablation、calibration baseline、N-scaling 到 N=32、bootstrap significance、TOST equivalence testing 等。\n\n**S6. 统计方法总体恰当。** Spearman ρ 用于 ordinal correlation、AUROC 用于 binary discrimination、sign test 用于 paired selection comparison、bootstrap CI 用于 signal comparison、TOST 用于 equivalence testing——工具选择合理且不过度。\n\n## 3. Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix\n\n**W1 [Must-Fix]. Few-NERD 3-epoch vs SciERC 5-epoch 的 confound 削弱了核心 cross-dataset comparison。**\n- **位置**: §4.1, §4.4 (Table 4), §6 Discussion\n- **问题**: 论文的核心叙事依赖 Few-NERD（low degeneracy → LP works）vs SciERC（high degeneracy → LP fails）的对比。但 Few-NERD 模型训练 3 epochs，SciERC 训练 5 epochs。Appendix N 的 epoch confound analysis 明确显示：Few-NERD 5-epoch degeneracy 从 11.1% 升至 31.3%，LP gains 从 +1.37pp 降至 +0.62pp。同时，SciERC 3-epoch degeneracy 仅 6.4%（远低于 Few-NERD 的 13.8%），但 LP 增益仍为 -1.18pp（Table 13）。\n- **这意味着**: (a) Table 4 的 cross-dataset gradient 是 training duration 和 dataset property 的混淆效应；(b) SciERC 3-epoch 的数据实际上 **反驳** 了 \"low degeneracy → LP works\" 的简单叙事（degeneracy 6.4% 但 LP Δ = -1.18pp），论文在 Appendix N 末段承认了这一点，但主文叙事未充分调整。\n- **为什么影响接收**: 这是论文的 central empirical claim 之一。Table 4 被 Introduction（§1, line 13-14）、Experiments（§4.4）、Discussion（§6）和 Conclusion（§7）反复引用。如果 cross-dataset gradient 被 epoch confound 污染，\"两个必要条件\" 的证据强度显著下降。\n- **建议**: (1) 在 Table 4 旁明确标注 training epochs 差异；(2) 加入 \"matched-epoch\" 对比行（如 SciERC 3-epoch vs Few-NERD 3-epoch，或两者都在 5-epoch 下对比）；(3) 如果 matched-epoch 数据不可得，在主文（不仅是 appendix）明确承认这一 confound 对 cross-dataset gradient 的影响，并降低相关 claim 的强度。\n\n**W2 [Must-Fix]. \"Two jointly necessary conditions\" 的因果性表述过强。**\n- **位置**: §1 Contribution 2-3, §4.4, §6 Discussion\n- **问题**: \"Jointly necessary\" 是一个强因果声明。论文的证据是 observational：不同 datasets、不同 training durations、不同 entity types 下的条件共变。Temperature ablation（§6, Table 11）是最接近 interventional evidence 的，但它只操纵了 degeneracy，alignment 是被动观察的（ρ=0.225 at T=1.2）。没有任何实验独立操纵 alignment 来验证其必要性。SciERC 3-epoch（Table 13: degeneracy 6.4%, ρ=0.317, LP Δ=-1.18pp）是一个 suggestive case，但 ρ=0.317 是否真的低于 \"threshold\"（论文建议 ρ≳0.3）是模糊的。\n- **为什么影响接收**: 如果审稿人将 \"jointly necessary\" 理解为严格的因果条件（必要条件意味着违反任一条件必定导致失败），那么 SciERC 3-epoch 的 case（ρ=0.317 > 0.3 且 degeneracy 6.4% < 15%，但 LP 仍为负）就是一个反例。\n- **建议**: 将 \"jointly necessary\" 改为 \"jointly associated with\" 或 \"both appear required in the regimes we tested\"。在主文讨论 SciERC 3-epoch 的 case 作为边界情况。ρ≳0.3 的 threshold 应标注为 approximate 和 data-driven，而非一个 sharp boundary。\n\n### Should-Fix\n\n**W3 [Should-Fix]. 2-seed 实验设计统计效力不足。**\n- **位置**: §4.1 footnote 5, Limitations §8 line 13\n- **问题**: Selection 的 primary results（Table 3）使用 2-seed 平均。论文在 footnote 5 承认 \"Three-seed experiments (σ≤0.026; Table 6) confirm ranking stability; the reduced seed count for primary results reflects computational budget.\" 但 2 seeds 无法估计 variance——σ from 2 points 的置信区间极宽。Table 6 的 3-seed stability 仅适用于 QE 的 signal rankings，不直接验证 selection gains 的 seed stability。\n- **建议**: 至少对 primary selection comparison（Table 3 中 LP vs greedy on SciERC）补充 3-seed 数据，或者更明确地标注 2-seed 结果的 uncertainty bounds。Few-NERD 的 +1.36pp 有 bootstrap CI [+1.18, +1.53]（within-seed），但 cross-seed uncertainty 未量化。\n\n**W4 [Should-Fix]. Per-entity-type 分析的 non-independence 问题比论文讨论的更严重。**\n- **位置**: §4.4, Figure 3, Contribution 3\n- **问题**: 8 entity types 来自同一模型、同一 tokenizer、同一 temperature、同一 training run。论文承认 \"effective degrees of freedom may be lower than 6\"，但未量化实际的 effective df。如果 types 之间高度相关（如 Person/Location/Organization 的 LP 行为可能受共享的 character-level tokenization 模式驱动），effective df 可能低至 2-3，此时 p=0.004 的 significance 消失。Appendix L (Table 10) 的 partial correlation 控制了 entity-type frequency（ρ=-0.47 vs raw -0.38），但未控制更重要的 confounders（如 type-level 的 token length distribution、schema complexity）。\n- **建议**: (1) 报告 entity types 之间 LP behavior 的 ICC（intraclass correlation），用于估计 effective df；(2) 如果 effective df 太低，将 p=0.004 标注为 nominal p-value 而非 corrected p-value；(3) 在 Figure 3 caption 和 Contribution 3 中明确 qualifying。\n\n**W5 [Should-Fix]. \"Supervised verifier baseline\" 的 framing 有误导性。**\n- **位置**: §4.5 Robustness, §6 Discussion\n- **问题**: §4.5 的 \"supervised verifier\" 实际上只是用 logistic regression/MLP/random forest 对 5 个 unsupervised scalar signals 进行 supervised combination。它不是 Cobbe et al. (2021) 意义上的 verifier（后者使用 hidden states 或 full representation 进行 learned scoring）。论文在 §4.5 末段承认 \"whether richer representations (e.g., hidden states, entity embeddings) could close the gap remains untested\"，但这个 acknowledgment 出现在 \"supervised verifiers... all perform at or below greedy\" 的声明之后，容易误导读者认为 supervised learning approach 已被排除。\n- **为什么影响评分**: 这影响 Conclusion 的 \"supervised combination of these five unsupervised signals does not close the oracle gap\"（§7 line 8）——这个结论是正确的但 framing 需要更精确。\n- **建议**: 将 \"supervised verifier baseline\" 改为 \"supervised signal combination\" 或 \"supervised aggregation baseline\"。在首次提及时立即澄清 scope：这测试的是 combination strategy，不是 learned representation。\n\n**W6 [Should-Fix]. Degeneracy 的操作性定义需要更清晰地在主文中区分。**\n- **位置**: Table 4 footnote, §4.3, Appendix D (Table 8)\n- **问题**: \"Instance degeneracy\" 被定义为 \"所有 N 个 samples 产生相同的 instance-level F1\"（Table 4 footnote），这与 \"所有 N 个 outputs 完全相同\" 不同。Table 8 显示 Qwen/SciERC 的 \"Identical outputs\" = 11%，但 \"Constant F1\" = 17.8%。这 6.8% 的差异（outputs 不同但 F1 相同）对选择问题有不同含义：outputs 不同意味着存在可选择的候选，但 F1 碰巧相同意味着选择不影响结果。这个区分在 Discussion（§6: \"14–59% of instances yield identical F1 scores\"）中被正确使用，但 Abstract 和 Introduction 中 \"identical F1 scores across all samples\" 容易被误读为 \"identical outputs\"。\n- **建议**: 在 §3.1 Problem Formulation 中给出明确定义，区分 output degeneracy（identical outputs）和 F1 degeneracy（identical F1, possibly different outputs），并说明为什么 F1 degeneracy 是 selection 问题的相关定义。\n\n**W7 [Should-Fix]. Table 4 中 SciERC 数值在不同 tables 之间存在不一致。**\n- **位置**: Table 3 vs Table 4 vs Table 12\n- **问题**: SciERC greedy F1 在 Table 3 为 0.645，Table 4 为 0.644，Table 12 为 0.640。Headroom 在 Table 3 为 13.3pp，Table 4 为 13.2pp。论文在 Table 4 footnote 解释了原因（\"max-LP aggregation and single-seed evaluation\"），Table 12 footnote 也解释了（\"gold-filtered evaluation... seed and aggregation differences\"）。但这些差异分散在不同 footnotes 中，对审稿人来说很难追踪哪个是 canonical 数字。\n- **建议**: 在某处（如 §4.1 或 Appendix A）统一声明 canonical evaluation protocol，列出每个 table 偏离 canonical protocol 的原因。或者更好的做法：统一 pipeline 使所有 tables 中同一 dataset 的 greedy F1 数值一致。\n\n### Minor\n\n**M1 [Minor].** Abstract line 5: \"LP achieves +1.36 pp on Few-NERD (p<0.001) under two jointly necessary conditions\" — \"jointly necessary\" 同 W2，建议弱化。\n\n**M2 [Minor].** §1 line 8: \"Unlike Yao et al. (2026), who demonstrate that self-consistency correlates with task accuracy, we investigate why this correlation does not reliably translate to selection gains\" — 定位准确，但 Yao et al. 使用 hard-prompted LLMs 而本文使用 fine-tuned models，这个差异应明确提及，因为 degeneracy 在 fine-tuned models 中更严重（论文在 Related Work §5 line 9-10 提到，但 Introduction 中未提及）。\n\n**M3 [Minor].** Table 3 的 EM 和 VC 没有报告 p-values（标注为 \"---\"）。如果是因为它们低于 greedy，应标注为 \"< greedy\" 或给出实际 p-value。\n\n**M4 [Minor].** §4.5 Robustness: Kendall's W=1.0 的 significance \"exact p≈0.028\" 基于 m=3 raters。论文承认 \"statistical precision is limited with m=3 raters\"，但仍在主文中报告 W=1.0。建议更 prominent 地标注这个 low-power caveat。\n\n**M5 [Minor].** References: Willard & Louf (2023) 被引用为 constrained decoding，但实际 inference 使用 vLLM 的 XGrammar（Appendix E line 498-500）。应同时引用 XGrammar。\n\n**M6 [Minor].** 论文未引用 Structured Prediction 相关的一些经典工作，如 McCallum et al. (2000) Maximum Entropy Markov Models 或 Lafferty et al. (2001) CRFs，这些在 confidence estimation for structured outputs 的文献中有直接相关性。此外 Selective Prediction 文献中 Geifman & El-Yaniv (2017) 也缺失。\n\n## 4. Questions\n\n**Q1.** SciERC 3-epoch 的 within-instance alignment ρ=0.317 略高于建议的 threshold ρ≳0.3，但 LP selection Δ=-1.18pp。这是否意味着 threshold 应该更高（如 ρ≳0.4 或 0.5），还是说 SciERC 存在 dataset-specific factors 使得 alignment threshold 不具有跨 dataset 的普适性？\n\n**Q2.** Few-NERD 是 INTRA coarse setting（8 coarse types），而非 fine-grained types。在 fine-grained types 下（更多 types → 可能更多 degeneracy），LP selection 是否仍然有效？这会影响结论的 generalizability。\n\n**Q3.** Greedy decoding (T=0) 作为 selection baseline 是否太弱？在实践中，practitioners 可能使用 beam search 或 top-k sampling with post-hoc heuristics。与这些更强的 baselines 对比，LP selection 的优势是否会进一步缩小？\n\n**Q4.** 论文报告了 LP 的 entity-token variant（§6: \"Entity-token LP exhibits 4× higher variance yet performs worse\"）。这是否说明 LP compression 的根本原因是 fine-tuned models 在 representation level 上 near-deterministic，而非 token-level averaging 或 schema-token dilution？如果是，这对 \"learned verifier\" 的可行性意味着什么——如果 hidden states 同样 near-deterministic，learned verifier 可能同样失败。\n\n**Q5.** Table 12 (N-scaling selection): 从 N=8 到 N=32，LP Δ 从 +0.39pp 增长到 +0.95pp（仍 p=0.091 不显著）。如果外推到 N=64 或 N=128，是否可能达到显著水平？这是否意味着 LP selection 在 SciERC 上只是 underpowered 而非 fundamentally limited？\n\n## 5. Missing References\n\n- Geifman & El-Yaniv (2017). \"Selective Prediction Using Deep Neural Networks.\" NeurIPS. — 与 selective prediction / abstention 直接相关。\n- Lafferty, McCallum, & Pereira (2001). CRFs — structured prediction 中 confidence 的经典基础。\n- XGrammar (实际使用的 constrained decoding engine) 应被引用。\n- Lin et al. (2024). \"Generating Confidence from LLMs.\" ACL 2024. — 与 verbalized confidence 和 LP-based confidence 直接比较。\n\n## 6. Overall Assessment\n\n## 总体评分\n**Overall**: 3.5 (borderline between Findings acceptance and Conference acceptance)\n**一句话总结**: 方法论框架（QE vs selection 的分离）和实验深度出色，但核心因果 claim（\"two jointly necessary conditions\"）被 epoch confound 和有限的 causal identification strategy 削弱。\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR review form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 实验设计总体严谨，统计方法恰当，透明度极高。但 epoch confound 对 cross-dataset comparison 的影响未在主文充分处理（W1），\"jointly necessary\" 的因果声明过强（W2），per-entity-type analysis 的 effective df 问题未量化（W4）。如果 W1 和 W2 被修复（降低 claim 强度 + 补充 matched-epoch 数据或承认 confound），soundness 可升至 4。 |\n| Excitement | 3.5/5 | QE vs selection 的分离是一个有洞察力的贡献，对 test-time scaling 社区有直接启示。但负结果论文（\"X 不 work\"）的 excitement 天然受限，且 4B-8B 的 scale coverage 在 2026 年显得有限。 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | Training config 详细（Appendix E），所有 hyperparameters 和 seeds 明确，constrained decoding setup 清楚。代码承诺 release。唯一不确定的是 Few-NERD 的 gold-filtering 具体实现。 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 我仔细阅读了全文和所有 appendix sections，验证了核心统计数字的内部一致性，搜索了 ACL ARR review form 标准。对 NER/IE 方法和统计方法有较深了解。 |\n\n---\n\n**总结**: 这是一篇实验深度远超平均水平的实证论文，transparency 令人印象深刻。核心贡献（correlation-selection gap 的诊断）是 sound 的。主要问题在于：(1) cross-dataset comparison 被 epoch confound 污染但主文未充分处理；(2) 因果 claim 的措辞过强于证据所能支撑。这两个问题都可以通过修改叙述强度和补充少量实验来修复，不需要从根本上改变论文结构。修复后，本文有潜力达到 main conference 水平。\n",
  "reviewer_id": "1",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie"
}
